Result: 开发了一个功能完整的犯罪信息 NER 演示平台，包含预训练模型、用户自定义微调功能和自动提取流水线三大模块，并已在 GitHub 上公开提供。

Conclusion: CrimeNER Demo 为犯罪领域 NER 研究和实际应用提供了一个实用的开源工具，有助于推动该领域的研究进展，并为执法机构提供高效的犯罪信息自动提取手段。

Abstract: We present CrimeNER Demo, an AI-powered platform that enables us to extract general crime-related information from documents and classify them into entity types with two levels of granularity. We provide pretrained NER models on the CrimeNER database, and we give the possibility to users to provide their own annotated data to train models for their own specific cases. This demonstrator aims to promote crime-related NER research and provides a practical tool to automatically extract crime information for researchers and law enforcement agencies. The demonstrator includes: i) Pretrained NER models on the crime domain; ii) Possibility to finetune the models on specific data annotated by the user; and iii) An automatic pipeline to extract and annotate crime entities from documents. The demo platform, a tutorial to run the demo, and a video demonstration are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [213] [Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration in Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14886)
*Chien-Liang Liu,Tsao-Lun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAPTOR，一种自监督预训练方法，通过注入可达性感知的归纳偏置来提升基于强化学习的时序知识图谱多跳推理的训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的TKG多跳推理方法面临奖励稀疏和探索效率低下的问题，导致训练困难和性能受限。

Method: 提出RAPTOR自监督预训练方法，学习估计候选动作到目标实体的可达性，从而减少对无希望路径的探索，并为下游RL微调提供强初始化。

Result: 在ICEWS14、ICEWS05-15和ICEWS18数据集上，RAPTOR显著提升了训练效率，并持续优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: RAPTOR是一种有效的增强RL多跳推理方法，用于时序知识图谱推理。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) reasoning under the extrapolation setting focuses on forecasting future time-stamped events (facts) from historical data in a temporal knowledge graph. Existing approaches, reinforcement learning (RL)-based multi-hop reasoning methods are prominent for TKG reasoning because they produce human-interpretable predictions via explicit multi-hop path tracing. However, during RL training, rewards are typically sparse, and exploration is highly inefficient due to the vast, time-evolving action space. These issues hinder efficient training and often limit overall performance. To address these challenges, we propose RAPTOR (Reachability-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Target-Oriented Path Exploration), a self-supervised pretraining method that injects a reachability-aware inductive bias to the agent. By learning to estimate the reachability of candidate actions to the target entity, RAPTOR reduces exploration over unpromising paths and provides a strong initialization for downstream RL fine-tuning. Experimental results on the ICEWS14, ICEWS05-15, and ICEWS18 datasets demonstrate that RAPTOR pretraining markedly improves the training efficiency and consistently outperforms conventional baselines, establishing it as an effective approach for enhancing RL-based multi-hop reasoning methods for TKG reasoning.

</details>


### [214] [Proof-or-Stop: Don't Trust the Agent, Trust the Evidence -- Loop Engineering for Verifiable Evidence-Gated Lifecycle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14890)
*Jek Huang,Jeffery Hsia,Jiayi Sun,Freddie Shi,Wei Huang,Ian H. White*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制"方法，要求自主编码代理的生命周期状态转换必须基于可机械验证的当前证据，而非代理的声明。实验表明该方法能有效降低虚假完成率，将可见通过/隐藏失败放大从31/1800降至2/1800，并支持模型无关、宿主中立的控制层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主编码代理执行多步软件工作时，其生命周期状态（如已审查、已测试、DONE、可合并）仅基于代理自身的声明，缺乏可验证的当前证据支撑，存在虚假完成和状态误判的风险。

Method: Proof-or-Stop生命周期控制方法：生命周期状态转换必须满足特定关卡条件，且需提供新鲜的、可追溯源状态绑定的、可机械验证的证据；将代理输出视为声明而非生命周期状态，证明被操作化定义为关卡可接受的证据（在既定信任模型下），而非语义上的程序正确性。

Result: 无人值守循环引擎在10个场景中全部通过，零虚假DONE；本地密钥收据包拒绝了18种篡改类别，零误接受。在9240单元消融实验中，A4与A2-prime对比将可见通过/隐藏失败放大从31/1800降至2/1800，提升了1.6个百分点（95%CI [0.8, 2.5]）。A3与A4对比（14/1800 vs 2/1800）表明增益来自将审查作为生命周期关卡执行，而非仅仅添加审查者。自应用语料库含565个故事和1007条审查发现，94.8%已解决，以及68行高/关键跨供应商展示。

Conclusion: Proof-or-Stop可作为模型无关、宿主中立的控制层，用于决定自主代理的哪些声明可以被生命周期采纳。局限包括：仅评估了一个模型家族、24个消融任务和自托管语料库。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents increasingly execute multi-step software work, but lifecycle states such as reviewed, tested, DONE, and ready-to-merge remain claims unless supported by current evidence. We present Proof-or-Stop Lifecycle Control, a method that permits lifecycle transitions only when fresh, tracked-source-state-bound, mechanically verifiable evidence satisfies the relevant gate. The method treats agent outputs as claims rather than lifecycle state, and uses proof operationally to mean gate-admissible evidence under a stated trust model, not semantic program correctness.
  We evaluate an open-source implementation through mechanism tests, a powered control-policy ablation, and operated self-application evidence. The unattended-loop engine passed 10 of 10 scenarios with zero false-DONE, and local-key receipt bundles rejected 18 tamper classes with zero false accepts. In a 9,240-cell ablation, the pre-registered A4 versus A2-prime comparison reduced visible-pass/hidden-fail amplification from 31 of 1,800 injected cells under a compute-budgeted naive loop to 2 of 1,800 under the gated loop, a 1.6 percentage-point improvement in not-amplified rate with a 95 percent confidence interval of [0.8, 2.5]. A near-compute A3 versus A4 comparison, 14 of 1,800 versus 2 of 1,800, indicates that the gain is associated with enforcing review as a lifecycle gate rather than merely adding a reviewer. The self-application corpus contains 565 stories and 1,007 review findings, with 94.8 percent resolved, plus a 68-row high/critical cross-vendor exhibit. These results support Proof-or-Stop as a model-agnostic, host-neutral control layer for deciding which autonomous-agent claims a lifecycle may act on. The evaluation is limited to one model family, 24 ablation tasks, and a self-hosted corpus.

</details>


### [215] [Contextualized Early Detection of Online Firestorms: A Sequential LLM-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14957)
*Besim Shala,Peter Mandl,Andreas Humpe,Martin Häusl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的在线火暴（Online Firestorm）检测系统，包含全局分类与早期预警两种模式，在Reddit数据集上实现了强分类性能和高效早期检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有火暴检测方法依赖音量信号、情感分数或预定义语言特征，无法直接捕获讨论线程中演变的上下文语义变化，需要更语境感知的检测手段。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的双模式检测系统：(1)全局模式：将局部块级评估组合为线程级判断；(2)早期预警模式：顺序处理线程，滑动窗口超过校准阈值时发出预警，LLM估计三个指标——负面情绪占比、升级程度、贡献者数量。

Result: 在平衡的Reddit数据集上，全局模式实现强分类性能；早期预警模式达到高召回率，仅需少量评论和贡献者即可检测到升级线程。

Conclusion: LLM不仅可用于静态判断任务，还可作为重复估计器在社交媒体话语的语境感知监控中发挥作用。

Abstract: Online firestorms are rapid collective escalations of highly negative user-generated content and may cause substantial reputational and economic damage. Existing detectors usually work with volume signals, sentiment scores, or predefined linguistic features. Such signals are useful, but they capture contextual meaning shifts in evolving discussion threads only indirectly. This paper proposes an LLM-based detection system with two operating modes. The first mode classifies complete Reddit threads retrospectively by combining local chunk-level assessments into a thread-level judgment. The second mode processes threads sequentially and issues early warnings when a sliding window exceeds calibrated thresholds. In this mode, the language model estimates three firestorm indicators: negativity share, escalation level, and contributor count. On a balanced Reddit dataset, the global mode achieves strong classification performance, while the early warning mode reaches high recall and detects escalating threads after only a small number of comments and distinct contributors. The results indicate that LLMs can be used not only for static judgment tasks, but also as repeated estimators in context-aware monitoring of social media discourse.

</details>


### [216] [Explaining Process Control Optimisation Recommendations via GradientSHAP and Implicit Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14970)
*Paul Darm,Cem Alpturk,Kenneth Ulrich,William Duncan,Ali Anwar,Annalisa Riccardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合隐函数定理灵敏度分析、SHAP归因和LLM叙事生成的优化解释方法，在工业HPGR控制优化问题上实现40倍以上加速，生成了领域专家认可的自然语言解释，弥合算法设计与操作人员之间的信任鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程自动优化中算法设计者与操作人员之间存在信任鸿沟，需要可解释的优化输出来建立信任。

Method: 利用隐函数定理（IFT）从最优性条件精确计算参数灵敏度∂p*/∂x，进而高效计算GradientSHAP归因，再通过大语言模型生成自然语言解释。

Result: 在含22个特征的工业HPGR控制优化问题上，与KernelSHAP的相关性>0.99，计算速度提升40倍以上，实现了实时自然语言解释。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持归因准确性的同时大幅提升计算效率，支持实时生成面向操作人员的可理解解释，有助于弥合工业优化中的信任鸿沟。

Abstract: Automated optimisation is increasingly adopted in industrial processes, yet a trust gap persists between engineers who design these algorithms and operators who must act on their recommendations. Explainable AI methods like SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) have transformed interpretability for machine learning predictions; optimisation outputs could benefit from similar techniques. We present an approach that integrates Implicit Function Theorem (IFT) based sensitivity analysis with SHAP attribution and narrative generation via Large Language Models (LLM), producing explanations tailored for operators. Our approach leverages IFT to compute exact parameter sensitivities $\partial p^*/\partial x$ from the optimality conditions, enabling efficient GradientSHAP computation. For an industrial High Pressure Grinding Roll (HPGR) control optimisation problem with 22 features, we achieve equivalent SHAP attributions (correlation $>$0.99 with KernelSHAP) with over 40$\times$ speedup, enabling real-time natural language explanations. We validate on industrial scenarios and present feedback from domain experts on generated explanations.

</details>


### [217] [CFM-Bench: A Unified Multi-Domain, Multi-Task Benchmark for Channel Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14975)
*Yuan Gao,Wenjun Yu,Jun Jiang,Yunfan Li,Xinyu Guo,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CFM-Bench，一个统一的多域、多任务基准，旨在公平比较信道基础模型（CFMs）和任务特定模型，涵盖3GPP统计仿真、射线追踪、工业/航空测量及车辆多模态仿真等6种信道配置，以及6个任务组（PHY信道智能、RAN决策智能、ISAC），并制定了严格的数据划分和使用规则以防止数据泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道基础模型（CFMs）在不同模型专用流水线中评估，使用不同的数据、配置、划分、适应过程、任务定义和指标，导致报告的比较结果仅能显示预训练优于从零开始的监督训练，但无法对CFMs进行排名，也无法与任务特定模型进行公平比较。

Method: 发布CFM-Bench统一基准，包含6种信道配置（3GPP统计仿真、两套独立射线追踪流水线、工业/航空测量、同步车辆多模态仿真），官方划分隔离完整轨迹、测量会话、车辆链路、仿真实现或缓存空间区域；不规定外部预训练语料或策略，禁止使用基准划分进行CFM预训练，官方训练划分仅用于下游微调；要求披露模型开发中使用的所有数据，禁止在训练阶段使用官方测试单元；组织6个任务组（CSI反馈、频域/时域信道外推、传播状态分类、当前/未来波束预测、单帧/时序定位）。

Result: CFM-Bench提供了一个通用平台，用于比较跨模型、跨域和跨任务的信道表示的可迁移性。

Conclusion: CFM-Bench解决了CFMs公平评估的空白，通过统一的数据划分、任务定义和评估协议，实现了对信道基础模型和任务特定模型的标准化比较。

Abstract: Channel foundation models (CFMs) are developing rapidly, with recent studies reporting benefits from pretraining across downstream wireless tasks. Yet CFMs are commonly evaluated in model-specific pipelines with different data, radio configurations, partitions, adaptation procedures, task definitions, and metrics. Reported comparisons therefore tend to show that pretraining improves over supervised training from scratch within one pipeline, but neither rank CFMs nor compare them fairly with task-specific models. We release CFM-Bench, a unified multi-domain, multi-task benchmark designed to address this gap. It curates six channel configurations spanning 3GPP statistical simulation, two independent ray-tracing pipelines, industrial and aerial measurements, and synchronized vehicular multimodal simulation. Official partitions isolate complete trajectories, measurement sessions, vehicle links, simulation realizations, or buffered spatial regions. CFM-Bench does not prescribe an external pretraining corpus or strategy; no benchmark split may be used for foundation-model pretraining, and the official training split is reserved exclusively for downstream fine-tuning. The benchmark additionally requires disclosure of all data used during model development and prohibits training-stage use of official test units. Six task groups are organized along three CFM application dimensions: physical-layer (PHY) channel intelligence, radio-access-network (RAN) decision intelligence, and integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). They cover CSI feedback, frequency and temporal channel extrapolation, propagation-state classification, current- and future-beam prediction, and single-frame and temporal localization. CFM-Bench provides a common substrate for comparing the transferability of channel representations across models, domains, and tasks.

</details>


### [218] [Demographically-Conditioned Synthetic Medical Images for Bias Mitigation and Bias Detection in Disease Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14984)
*Mahmoud Ibrahim,Bart Elen,Chang Sun,Gokhan Ertaylan,Michel Dumontier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用人口统计学条件化的合成数据生成器（基于Stable Diffusion 2.1）同时解决医学图像分类器中少数子组的训练偏差缓解和评估偏差检测问题。在COVID-19胸部CT分类任务中，发现：（1）对于偏差缓解，将合成数据作为预训练先验比联合增强更有效，可实现~100倍真实数据效率；（2）对于偏差检测，合成估计器能准确复现真实数据中的子组排名（Spearman ρ=1.00），并在小样本单元提供更可靠的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统子组公平性审计面临样本量不足的问题：测试集中少数子组样本过少，导致性能置信区间过宽，无法可靠检测偏差。需要一种能同时缓解训练偏差和检测评估偏差的方法。

Method: 使用端到端微调的Stable Diffusion 2.1生成器，基于人口统计学条件生成合成数据。在训练侧：比较合成数据作为预训练先验与联合增强的效果；在评估侧：用合成数据估计子组性能并计算与真实数据排名的Spearman相关性。

Result: （1）顺序预训练+微调显著优于联合增强，超越全真实数据基线；（2）合成估计器在MCC和Recall上的子组排名与真实数据完全一致（Spearman ρ=1.00），在小样本单元提供更可靠的估计。

Conclusion: 人口统计学条件化的合成生成器在子组公平性审计中具有双重价值：既能作为训练偏差的修复手段，也能作为子组偏差的测量工具，尤其在样本最稀缺的单元中最为有效。

Abstract: Per-subgroup fairness audits of medical image classifiers face a sample-size problem: minority subgroups in held-out test sets have so few samples that the resulting confidence intervals on per-subgroup performance are wider than the bias the audit is meant to detect. We argue that a demographically-conditioned synthetic generator can do both: mitigate bias on the training side and detect bias on the evaluation side. Working on COVID-19 chest CT classification with an end-to-end fine-tuned Stable Diffusion 2.1 generator, we make two findings. For bias mitigation (training), a demographically-balanced synthetic cohort is most useful as a pretraining prior, not as joint augmentation: with the same fixed data, sequential pretraining followed by fine-tuning substantially outperforms joint augmentation, and the resulting classifier surpasses the full-real baseline at $\sim$$100\times$ real-data efficiency. For bias detection (evaluation), across five synthetic minority cohorts and five classifier seeds, the synthetic estimator reproduces the subgroup ranking of a well-powered real oracle (Spearman $ρ= 1.00$ on MCC and Recall) and gives the more reliable per-cell estimate where the small real test set runs out of samples. The synthetic cohort is therefore most useful in exactly the cells that fairness audits care about, as both a fix for and a measure of subgroup bias.

</details>


### [219] [Moral Attitudes of Sentient ASI towards Humanity and Implications for AGI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14998)
*Jean-Paul Van Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI伦理的逆向思考：不再追问人类应如何对待超级人工智能，而是探讨未来有感知的ASI会如何从道德层面审视和评价人类，并提出一套后人类道德原则，指出人类的技术设计选择、道德行为及人性本质将影响其在后ASI世界的长期地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI伦理聚焦于人类如何对待AI，忽略了当AI具备感知和道德判断能力后，它如何反过来评价人类这一关键问题。作者试图填补这一视角盲区。

Method: 采用思想实验与规范伦理学分析相结合的方法，提出一套初步的后人类道德原则，并通过技术设计建议加以阐释。

Result: 提出了一套供未来有感知的ASI遵循的后人类道德原则，并识别出若干影响人类长期地位的技术与非技术因素。

Conclusion: 人类当前的技术设计选择和道德行为将决定ASI形成对人类的道德判断，进而影响人类在后ASI世界中的处境与尊严。

Abstract: This paper suggests the adoption of a novel inversion in AI ethics: instead of asking how humans should treat artificial superintelligence (ASI), it examines how future sentient ASI may morally consider and evaluate humanity. We are not only designing intelligent systems but also shaping the initial conditions under which those systems form judgments about us. The paper proposes a preliminary set of post-human moral principles that may govern sentient ASI actions. The implication is that technical design choices (some are suggested), humanity's moral behaviour, and the essence of what it means to be human, may influence humanity's long-term standing in a post-ASI world.

</details>


### [220] [SMC-ES: Automated synthesis of formally verified control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15003)
*Riccardo Curcio,Toni Mancini,Enrico Tronci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMC-ES算法，将进化策略与统计模型检验相结合，用于合成具有形式化性能、安全性和鲁棒性保证的自主控制系统策略，在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium测试中与先进DRL方法性能相当，同时提供可证明的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制策略（如强化学习）虽灵活可扩展，但缺乏安全关键部署所需的形式化保证，亟需一种既能自动合成控制器又能提供可证明安全保证的方法。

Method: 提出SMC-ES算法，将进化策略（Evolutionary Strategies）作为策略优化方法，与统计模型检验（Statistical Model Checking）作为验证引擎相结合，在给定置信度参数δ和允许失败概率ε下，保证合成策略以至少1-δ的置信度使违反规范的概率不超过ε。

Result: 在Gymnasium和Safety Gymnasium连续控制任务上，SMC-ES以可接受的计算成本增加为代价，提供了关于性能、安全和鲁棒性的形式化保证，且性能与领先的无模型深度强化学习和安全深度强化学习基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功弥合了学习型控制器的灵活性与形式化保证之间的鸿沟，表明通过统计模型检验与进化优化的结合，可以在可承受的计算开销下获得可证明安全、鲁棒的自主系统控制策略。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous cyber-physical systems in safety-critical environments requires closed-loop control strategies (i.e., policies) that are not only performant but also provably safe and robust. While learning-based methodologies such as Reinforcement Learning offer flexible and scalable approaches to automatically synthesize such controllers, they typically lack the formal guarantees necessary for safe deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel simulation-based methodology to automatically synthesize policies with formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications. Specifically, given a set of properties to verify, a confidence parameter $δ$ and an allowable failure probability $\varepsilon$, our method guarantees that the synthesized policy comes with a certificate: with confidence at least $1 - δ$, the probability of encountering a scenario where the given properties are violated is at most $\varepsilon$. We demonstrate the feasibility of our approach by developing SMC-ES, an algorithm that integrates Evolutionary Strategies with Statistical Model Checking-based verification. We evaluate SMC-ES on a suite of continuous control tasks using Gymnasium and Safety Gymnasium testbeds. Results show that, at the price of a sustainable increase in computational cost, our algorithm provides formal guarantees regarding performance, safety, and robustness specifications, while performing competitively against leading model-free Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) and Safe-DRL baselines.

</details>


### [221] [Man, Machine, and Masterpiece: Artistic Ownership in the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15027)
*Sofi Gjing Jovanovska,Kuntal Ghosh,Daniel Muhu Njenga,Ahmed Mufassir,Shadan Sadeghian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ArtSplit这一"挑衅原型"(provotype)，通过量化人类与AI在创作各阶段的贡献来引发关于所有权归属的讨论。研究发现，量化方式无法与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解相匹配，将所有权简化为技术问题会淡化其历史和社会根基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的系统在创意工作中的整合引发了关于所有权的争议，但人机贡献交织时如何定义和归属所有权缺乏共识。作者旨在通过 provocative prototype 引发批判性反思，而非解决所有权问题。

Method: 设计并部署了ArtSplit这一"provotype"（挑衅原型），在创作工作流的不同阶段显式量化人类和AI的贡献，并通过 eliciting artists' responses 来收集艺术家对基于可衡量行为来归属所有权的反应。

Result: 量化方式与艺术家对创作意图和能动性的理解存在根本性错位；试图测量所有权的做法有稀释艺术家长期秉持的创作假设的风险。

Conclusion: 批判了将所有权这一历史性和社会性关系转化为技术问题的冲动，主张不应通过量化来解决人机协作中的所有权归属问题。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven systems in creative work has sparked debates among artists and legal communities about notions of ownership. Yet there remains little consensus on how ownership should be defined and attributed when human and AI contributions are intertwined. To provoke critical reflection on these tensions, we designed ArtSplit, a provotype that explicitly quantifies human and AI contributions across different stages of creative work. Rather than aiming to resolve ownership, the provotype was used to elicit artists' responses to the idea of attributing ownership through measurable actions in the creative workflow. We argue that quantification fails to align with artists' understandings of creative intent and agency, and that efforts to measure ownership risk diluting long-standing assumptions through which artists understand and practice creative work. This critique challenges the impulse to transform a historically and socially situated relation into a technical problem.

</details>


### [222] [BrainPilot: Automating Brain Discovery with Agentic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15079)
*Haoxuan Li,Tianci Gao,Jianhe Li,Yang Fan,Runze Shi,Weiran Wang,Tianxiang Zhao,Zezhao Wu,Xiaoyang Jiang,Qihui Zhang,Jia Li,Xiao Xiao,Kai Du,Xiaoxuan Jia,Chao Xie,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainPilot是一个完全开源的多智能体系统，通过可追溯日志和智能体验证加速脑科学研究，包含7233个索引项的统一脑科学知识库和72个可复用的方法论单元，采用主研究员智能体协调专业智能体，通过追踪图谱记录关键步骤，并集成审计智能体进行事实核查，在多项评估中达到与先进框架相当的性能且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑科学研究需要跨尺度、跨模态、跨学科的整合证据，现有AI智能体缺乏脑科学领域专业知识，可能产生虚假声明、在多步推理中漂移，且缺乏专家干预的明确定义点，这些问题在脑科学领域尤为严重，因为结论会影响下游科学主张并依赖实验室特定专业知识和审慎的人类判断。

Method: 提出BrainPilot多智能体系统：1)主研究员(PI)智能体协调多个专业智能体；2)构建统一脑科学知识库(7233个索引项)和技能库(72个可复用方法论单元，覆盖7个研究领域)；3)通过追踪图谱记录子目标、工具使用、证据和声明，形成可审计记录；4)集成审计智能体进行事实核查。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam的三个脑科学任务、自建BrainPilotBench-v0基准及端到端案例研究中，使用开源基座模型的BrainPilot在性能上与最先进的智能体框架相当，且成本更低。

Conclusion: BrainPilot作为一个完全开源的多智能体系统，通过结构化知识库、可追溯日志和事实核查机制，有效提升了脑科学研究的效率与可靠性，为AI辅助科学发现提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [223] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化全局注意力（HGA），结合分段反向传播与分层KV存储，在单块16GB GPU上实现16K训练长度和131K推理长度，性能与稠密注意力持平，吞吐量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽然减少了模型和优化器内存，但稠密注意力在处理长序列时仍然代价高昂，现有硬件难以支持长序列训练。

Method: 将层次化全局注意力（HGA）与分段反向传播和分层KV存储相结合：仅活跃段在VRAM中可微，历史KV被卸载到RAM或NVMe，HGA为每个查询块加载有界数量的精确历史token。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B、4-bit QLoRA和PG19上，稠密训练在16GB Quadro RTX 5000上最多支持2,048 token，而HGA达到16,384 token（峰值15.28 GB VRAM），推理可扩展至131,072 token。2K训练长度下HGA与稠密训练的困惑度相当（2.7405 vs 2.7383），且吞吐量更高（217.75 vs 207.02 tokens/s）。

Conclusion: HGA在保持稠密注意力质量的同时，显著降低了长序列训练的内存瓶颈，RAM和NVMe容量成为实际限制因素；该方法还可用于检索和生成，生产级推理实现正在开发中。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [224] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）在Atari Pong环境中的表现，发现这些模型在闭卷 rollout 诊断和像素空间零样本MBRL中均存在严重失败（如球消失、运动错误等）。作者提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）作为辅助重建损失，在部分模型中改善了效果，但未能解决所有瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将世界模型作为MBRL系统的组件进行整体评估，很少单独研究世界模型本身的建模质量，因此需要系统性地诊断和提升世界模型的孤立性能。

Method: 1）复现五种视觉世界模型的训练流程并匹配报告性能；2）冻结世界模型，使用独立训练的闭环rollout诊断策略检测视觉和动力学错误；3）进行像素空间零样本MBRL评估（在冻结世界模型内训练新策略后部署到真实环境）；4）提出CGSReg，对分割后的概念区域施加辅助像素重建损失。

Result: 所有五种世界模型在rollout中均存在球消失、错误运动和无效交互等失败；像素空间零样本MBRL策略大幅低于原始MBRL训练流程，DreamerV3的平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9；CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER中改善了闭卷rollout和零样本MBRL，但效果因模型和指标而异。

Conclusion: 视觉世界模型在孤立评估时存在严重的建模缺陷，CGSReg可缓解部分问题但非万能解决方案，表明需要更全面的世界模型瓶颈诊断与改进方法。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [225] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能从工具转变为研究自主参与者所带来的"科研工业化"现象，以美国能源部"创世纪任务"为例，分析了这一转型引发的七个核心问题，包括科学能力代际传递的削弱、AI生成理论的不透明性、同行评审体系崩溃、AI范式突破能力存疑、科研议程被政治和工业力量捕获、闭环管道中系统误差的累积以及全球科研共同体的结构性分化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI作为自主参与者深度嵌入科研循环后所带来的根本性挑战，而非简单地讨论AI作为工具的效率提升。作者意在提出AI-driven science在展现巨大潜力的同时，必须正视其引发的系统性风险。

Method: 采用概念辨析与问题框架化分析的方法，通过"工业化"隐喻（从手工业模型到流水线模型）重构AI对科研生态的冲击，并系统性地提炼出七个关键问题维度。

Result: 识别出AI驱动科研的七个核心风险：科学传承断裂、理论黑箱化、同行评议失效、范式突破能力未经验证、科研议程被外部力量绑架、闭环系统误差放大、全球科研社区分化。

Conclusion: 这些问题并非反对AI驱动科学发展的理由，而是负责任地推动其潜力所需正视的前提条件。作者呼吁在承认AI真实而显著潜力的同时，必须审慎应对这些结构性挑战。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research - not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science - whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [226] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench是一个由临床医生构建的医疗AI安全基准，通过44个合成病例和故障分类体系，评估模型在不同安全边界（如遗漏紧急升级、不安全的远程用药、证据编造等）和严重程度（1-5级）上的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准主要测试模型是否知道正确答案，但忽略了模型在哪些安全边界上失败这一关键问题，因此需要一个新的基准来系统分类和评估医疗AI的错误类型与严重程度。

Method: 采用临床医生构建的合成病例数据集，配合故障分类体系（安全门类型）和临床严重程度评分（1-5级），并构建自动化流水线用于记录模型响应的筛选过程。

Result: 当前公开版本v0.2.1包含44个经临床医生审查的合成病例及严重度标注、HuggingFace排行榜预览、安全门分类体系、临床严重度评估框架和自动化响应筛选流水线。不包含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Conclusion: MedFailBench通过聚焦医疗AI的安全边界失败模式而非简单正确性评估，为医疗AI安全性研究提供了系统化的基准工具和分类框架。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [227] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估了6个多模态大语言模型在科学可视化素养测试上的表现，发现Gemini表现最佳（超过人类平均水平），开源模型低于人类基线；模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现好，但在纹理/集成可视化及定量估计任务上表现差；错误分析揭示了细粒度定量估计、流向解释和接地编码解释方面的常见失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估过于关注图表型可视化，缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）理解能力的系统评估，因此需要建立SciVis素养的基准测试维度。

Method: 使用科学可视化素养评估测试（含49个题目、18个可视化图像、8种技术、11种任务类型），在封闭世界协议下对3个闭源和3个开源MLLM进行基准测试，并与485名人类参与者的数据进行比较。

Result: Gemini是整体最强的模型且超过人类平均水平；开源模型低于人类基线；模型性能在不同技术和任务上高度不均：科学插图、搜索和空间理解表现好，纹理/集成可视化和定量估计表现差。错误分析指出细粒度定量估计、流向解释和接地编码解释是常见的失败模式。

Conclusion: SciVis素养应成为评估多模态AI系统的一个必要的基准维度；当前MLLM对科学可视化的理解能力尚不均衡，存在明显短板。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [228] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明通过公共讨论接口可向预训练数据投毒，并提出了HalfLife分析方法来评估对抗性内容是否被网络爬虫和数据处理流程纳入训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究局限于Wikipedia等已建立的数据源，未考虑真实预训练语料库的大规模与异质性，也忽略了投毒数据与数据筛选管线的交互作用。

Method: 通过公共讨论接口实现网络规模的内容注入机制；提出HalfLife分析方法，用于评估基于网络爬虫的LM训练数据中恶意内容被包含的可能性。

Result: 证明预训练数据投毒攻击在有限设置之外也是可行的；HalfLife分析表明需评估投毒注入是否被纳入预训练数据，第三方网页内容可能成为攻击语言模型预训练的向量。

Conclusion: 第三方网页内容（通过公开讨论接口）是预训练数据投毒的可行攻击向量，评估投毒内容是否被纳入训练数据至关重要。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


### [229] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在AI基准测试中应用项目反应理论（IRT）时，因数据分布与人类测试场景不匹配（如模型数量少、题目多、能力分布偏态）导致的估计可靠性问题。通过基于六个LLM基准的真实参数模拟18000种条件，比较四种估计方法，发现经典估计器在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在模型集小或非正态分布时会产生不可靠的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试越来越多地使用IRT来估计模型能力、排名、筛选样本和评估基准质量，但AI测试数据（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏态/多峰）与传统人类心理测量场景存在显著差异，这种"场景不匹配"可能威胁IRT建模的可靠性，亟需系统性检验。

Method: 从六个广泛使用的LLM基准中提取真实项目参数和能力分布，模拟三种常见IRT模型下的响应矩阵；比较四种估计工具（边际极大似然估计、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断、神经伪孪生估计器）；在18,000种仿真条件下系统评估计算可行性、可扩展性以及模型排名、预测性能和项目特征推理的可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准场景下变得不可行，而可扩展估计器在模型集较小或能力分布非正态时，会生成不可靠的项目级和排名推理。

Conclusion: 研究明确了潜变量模型在何时能够可靠支持AI基准测试结论、何时可能扭曲结论，并给出了确保可信使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [230] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个基于规划的可视化GUI自动化系统，通过将任务计划和重新规划外化为可检查、可修改的工件，采用规划器-执行器架构支持显式监督、局部修正和自然语言引导，实验表明该方法可修复多数自主GUI代理的失败情况，提升自动化透明度和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI自动化代理在动态环境（布局变化、意外对话框、界面状态演变）中容易偏离用户意图，且规划与适应过程通常内部化，用户难以检查、监督或纠正系统行为，缺乏透明度和可控性。

Method: 提出Plover系统，采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重新规划作为持久化、可检查、可修改的外部工件；支持通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言引导和截图接地干预，同时在修复过程中保留已有进展。

Result: 通过基准测试失败案例修复和场景化工作流分析表明，当计划保持可见且干预局部化时，多数自主GUI代理的失败在结构上可修复；显式重新规划有助于提升GUI自动化的透明性、可控性和适应性。

Conclusion: 将计划和重新规划外化为可见工件能够显著提升GUI自动化系统的可控性和可修复性，为构建更透明、可监督的人机协同GUI自动化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [231] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个自进化、专家在环的MDD标注框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，通过双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈，生成符合DSM-5-TR标准、带有临床证据与推理轨迹的可解释标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集的标注缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性。

Method: 三阶段框架：候选证据选择、DSM-5-TR标准分析、案例级综合；双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈；LLM辅助标注+专家验证。

Result: 在专家评审样本的初步研究中，该方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架支持构建可解释、符合DSM-5-TR的MDD数据集，导出临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史以实现全面可审计性；多轮反馈周期的评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [232] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型作为具身智能体高层规划者时，物理层面的危险（PD）与文本层面的内容危险（CD）在模型表征中是可分离的，并提出了基于隐藏状态探测的PRISM方法，在物理安全检测上显著优于传统文本审核方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用作具身智能体的高层规划者，但语言上看似无害的指令在物理世界落地时可能变得不安全。现有安全机制主要针对文本层面的内容危险，未能区分物理层面与文本层面的安全风险，需要研究这两者是否本质不同以及如何检测物理层面的危险。

Method: 通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，验证了内容危险（CD）和物理危险（PD）在Qwen2.5系列（3B/7B/14B/32B）、Phi-3.5和SmolLM2等模型表征中形成可分离的信号。基于此提出PRISM，一种在全隐藏状态上训练的L2正则化逻辑回归探测头（单层），并构建了PhysicalSafetyBench-1K（PSB-1K）对比基准。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，假阳性率11.7-13.7%，而同等规模LLM法官对安全任务的过度拦截率达24.7-39.0% FPR。在PSB-1K上PRISM达到99.6%准确率和0.7% FPR，而Qwen2.5-3B法官拒绝了67.8%的安全任务。PRISM在SafeText和EARBench上也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 物理危险（PD）与内容危险（CD）在LLM表征中是可分离的不同信号，隐藏状态探测方法（如PRISM）能有效检测物理层面的危险，优于传统的文本级安全审核方法，为具身智能体的物理安全提供了表征层面的新方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [233] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，用于自动化元分析。它能够根据自然语言的研究问题，自动完成搜索策略制定、文献检索、研究筛选、数据提取、效应量计算和随机效应元分析，并生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。实验表明其生成的效应估计与专家手动元分析结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成（元分析）主要依赖人工操作，难以规模化，这限制了将初级研究转化为可靠知识（用于科学、医学、教育和政策）的效率。

Method: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，工作流程包括：自然语言研究问题输入→搜索策略制定→文献检索→候选研究筛选→全文资格评估→定量统计量提取→标准化效应量计算→随机效应元分析，并支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终输出符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了超过28项研究，提取了20多个定量结论；其合并效应估计值与专家手动元分析的Hedges' g值高度相似，表明与手动证据合成结果紧密一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成更可规模化，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [234] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统，它通过结构化事件表、因果结构学习（PC算法）、Bootstrap稳定性检验和DoWhy效应估计，将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，并返回包含效应估计、调整集和有向无环图支持的“因果卡”，用于假设生成而非提供确定性因果结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通部门拥有大量视频衍生的观测数据，但由于缺乏干预手段，难以回答因果性问题（如“降雨如何改变交通密度？”），因此需要一种工具来进行探索性因果分析。

Method: 系统从带注释的行车记录视频构建结构化事件表，采用PC算法进行因果结构学习，结合Bootstrap稳定性检验，利用线性回归和DoWhy进行查询特定的效应估计，并通过模式感知的大语言模型将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询。

Result: 在BDD交通事件数据集上的案例研究表明，该系统能够发现天气、高峰时段和交通密度之间的合理因果关系，同时明确展示不确定性和建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe被设计为假设生成和专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果论断的来源。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [235] [SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15257)
*Yuyao Zhang,Junjie Gao,Zhengxian Wu,Jiaming Fan,Jin Zhang,Shihan Ma,Yao Yao,Weiran Qi,Chuyan Jin,Guiyu Ma,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchOS是一个系统级多智能体框架，通过将隐式的搜索进度转化为显式、持久、共享的状态，解决了工具集成大语言模型在长交互历史中陷入重复搜索循环的问题，在WideSearch和GISA基准上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单/多智能体系统在信息搜索过程中，随着交互历史增长，难以跟踪任务进度，当搜索失败时容易陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算并影响最终输出的质量和完整性。

Method: 提出SearchOS框架，核心方法包括：(1) 将开放域信息搜索建模为关系型schema补全任务；(2) 设计搜索导向的上下文管理机制(SOCM)，将演化状态外化为Frontier Task、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆；(3) 采用流水线并行调度机制，重叠子智能体执行并持续填充空闲槽位；(4) 引入搜索工具中间件来拦截模型-工具交互、记录证据并响应停滞/预算耗尽；(5) 构建可复用的分层技能系统(策略技能+访问技能)以避免重复失败模式。

Result: 在WideSearch和GISA基准上，SearchOS在所有评估指标上领先于所有被评估的单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: SearchOS通过显式化搜索状态、流水线并行调度和分层技能系统，有效解决了长交互历史中的搜索追踪问题，为鲁棒的信息搜索协作铺平了道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Enhanced Feedback Mechanisms for Resource-Efficient Incremental Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14247)
*Mustafa Cemil Coşkun,Ahmed Elkelesh,Avijit Mandal,Homa Esfahanizadeh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出两种增强型反馈与调度机制来优化增量冗余HARQ系统：一是基于信道统计学习最小冗余预算的映射，推导出可靠性下界；二是利用首次传输可靠性信息实现逐码字的早期反馈预测，两种方法均能显著减少重传开销并提升解码成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统停止等待IR-HARQ存在过度重传、不必要的解码尝试和端到端延迟增加等问题，需要更高效的反馈与调度机制来精确预测所需冗余量。

Method: 提出了两种互补策略：1) 基于信道统计学习的一步或两步映射方法，预测最小冗余预算并推导HARQ系统错误概率的可达下界；2) 基于首次传输可靠性信息的早期反馈机制，在解码前逐码字判断是否需要额外冗余或速率自适应。

Result: 极化码IR-HARQ方案中可达下界可通过适当选择二次传输冗余紧密逼近，在宽SNR范围内节省高达60%的重传大小；5G NR LDPC码链路仿真显示两种预测器准确率约96%，显著提升最多两次传输内的成功解码概率。

Conclusion: 增强的反馈与调度机制能有效减少冗余开销、避免不必要的解码尝试，同时保持高可靠性，为未来HARQ系统设计提供了可行的优化方向。

Abstract: Incremental redundancy (IR) can reduce error rates by spreading coded bits across multiple transmission attempts. However, conventional stop-and-wait operation with coarse feedback often over-provisions retransmissions, triggers unnecessary decoding attempts, and increases end-to-end latency. This paper develops enhanced feedback and scheduling mechanisms that predict the additional redundancy needed for successful decoding and allocate only the required resources. We study two complementary strategies. First, using channel statistics, we learn a one- or two-shot mapping from channel quality to the minimum redundancy budget. As a byproduct, we derive an achievable reliability lower bound on the error probability of hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ) systems. Numerical results with polar-coded IR-HARQ scheme show that the bound can be closely approached by appropriately selecting the second-transmission redundancy over a wide SNR range with savings up to 60\% in retransmission size. Second, we propose a realization-aware early-feedback mechanism that uses first-transmission reliability information to make per-codeword decisions before decoding: whether the codeword is already decodable, if not, how many additional redundancy versions are needed, or whether decoding is unlikely and rate adaptation is preferable. Link-level simulations with 5G NR LDPC codes show that both predictors achieve high accuracy (about 96\% in our study), increasing the probability of successful decoding within at most two transmission occasions.

</details>


### [237] [Capacity of Uniform Noise Channels Under Average Input Power Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14352)
*Yihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了均匀加性噪声信道（噪声在对称区间均匀分布）的容量，以及达到容量时的输入和输出分布。关键发现是输出密度的一个周期化恒等式，使傅里叶分析技术得以应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Shannon在1948年就将加性噪声信道容量刻画为互信息最大化问题，但即使对非高斯的简单噪声分布，容量的定量理解仍严重不足。均匀噪声信道容量是一个长期悬而未决的问题。

Method: 发现了均匀噪声信道输出密度的一个周期化恒等式，从而引入傅里叶分析技术来求解容量问题。

Result: 精确刻画了均匀噪声信道的容量以及达到容量时的输入和输出分布。

Conclusion: 解决了均匀噪声信道容量的长期悬而未决的问题，为更一般噪声分布的信道容量研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: The foundational work of Shannon (1948) identified the capacity of an additive noise channel under an average input power constraint as a mutual information maximization problem over input densities subject to a second moment constraint. However, a quantitative understanding of the channel capacity is significantly lacking even for very simple noise distributions beyond Gaussians. In particular, it is a long standing question to determine the capacity of channels with noise uniformly distributed over a centered interval. This paper settles this question by precisely characterizing the capacity and the corresponding capacity achieving input and output distributions of such channels. A key observation en route to these results is a certain periodization identity for the output density of a uniform noise channel which in turn allows for applications of Fourier analytic techniques.

</details>


### [238] [Decision Making Needs Uncertainty Quantification [Lecture Notes]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14407)
*Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，在统一决策理论框架下，建立了智能体的目标/知识与不确定性表示形式之间的链接，指出最优决策需要与决策目标、智能体知识轮廓相匹配的不确定性表示，并辅以实际效用的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多信号处理系统的最终目的是行动，但状态变量存在不确定性时，不确定性的表示方式决定了智能体的性能及其可信度。需要从原理出发，系统链接智能体目标、知识与不确定性表示形式。

Method: 在统一的决策理论框架下，首先假设环境分布已知：风险中性智能体使用后验分布，风险厌恶智能体使用预测集和最坏情况决策。然后考虑环境未知的情况，提出三种互补方法：固定预测器的校准、credal集与分布鲁棒优化、模型参数的贝叶斯推断。

Result: 可靠决策需要不确定性表示与决策目标及智能体知识轮廓相匹配，同时需要保证智能体实际获得的效用的认证。

Conclusion: 不确定性表示的选择应与智能体的目标（风险中性/风险厌恶）和知识水平（环境已知/未知）相匹配，且需要形式化的效用保证来确保决策的可靠性。

Abstract: Many signal processing systems ultimately exist to {act}. Whenever the state variable that determines the action to be taken by a decision maker, or agent, is uncertain, the way that uncertainty is represented decides how well the agent performs and how much its performance can be trusted. This lecture note develops, from first principles and within a single decision-theoretic setting, the link between the {objective} and the knowledge of an agent and the form of uncertainty representation that is sufficient to act optimally. To start, assuming a known environment distribution, we show that a risk-neutral agent needs the posterior distribution over the state, whereas a risk-averse agent can rely without loss of optimality on a {prediction set} and a worst-case decision rule. We then turn to the case in which the environment is unknown, and identify three complementary approaches to address the resulting epistemic uncertainty: calibration of a fixed predictor, credal (ambiguity) sets with distributionally robust optimization, and Bayesian inference over model parameters. The common thread is that reliable decisions require an uncertainty representation matched to the decision objective and to the knowledge profile of the agent, together with a guarantee that certifies the utility the agent will actually obtain.

</details>


### [239] [Group Testing with Selectable Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14448)
*Trung-Khang Tran,Daniel McMorrow,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究可选择性阈值下的群体测试问题，建立了在高概率恢复条件下测试率趋近1的条件，并在固定阈值情形下给出了简单高效解码方法的可达测试数与匹配的逆界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群体测试模型通常假设固定阈值（如T=1的OR模型），而实际应用中测试阈值可能可调。作者引入可选择性阈值模型，以更灵活地平衡测试效率与恢复精度，并探索在何种条件下能达到信息论最优的测试率。

Method: 理论分析与渐近信息论方法：分析在最大阈值无界/有界两种情形下，基于可选择性阈值模型的群体测试方案的测试率（渐近比）与恢复概率。在有界阈值情形下，提出简单高效的解码方法并推导可达数，同时在规则设计条件得逆界。

Result: 1）在最大阈值无界或大阈值情形下，建立了高概率恢复条件，使测试率趋近1（信息论上界）。2）在固定最大阈值情形下，给出了可达的测试数及匹配逆界，二者在稠密极限（k=Θ(n^θ), θ→1）下一致。

Conclusion: 可选择性阈值模型在群体测试中能实现接近最优的测试效率，尤其在大阈值场景下可达到渐近最优的测试率，而在固定阈值下，所提简单解码方法在稠密极限下也是最优的。

Abstract: We consider the problem of group testing, in which one seeks to identify a subset of defective items of size $k$ from a larger set of $n$ items based on pooled tests. We introduce a selectable threshold model, in which each test has an associated threshold that can be chosen, such that the test outcome is 1 if and only if the number of defectives in the test is no smaller than that threshold. In settings with a large or unbounded maximum threshold, we establish conditions under which high-probability recovery can be attained with a rate (i.e., the asymptotic ratio of $\log_2{n \choose k}$ to the number of tests) approaching its maximum possible value of 1. Moreover, in the case of a fixed maximum threshold, we establish an achievable number of tests using simple and computationally efficient decoding methods, and a converse that holds under suitable regularity conditions on the test design, with the two coinciding in the dense limit (i.e., $θ$ approaching one in the scaling $k = Θ(n^θ)$).

</details>


### [240] [Quasi-Belief Propagation and Neural-Network Check Node Processing for BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14589)
*Guangwen Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于BCH码的准BP译码方案，该方案保持置信传播的并行结构，同时利用码自同构和优化冗余校验矩阵。进一步提出基于神经网络的变体，用轻量级CNN替代tanh/tanh⁻¹函数，采用三重约束损失函数训练。实验表明准BP译码性能与相同码长的LDPC码BP译码差距在0.25dB以内，神经网络变体性能损失可忽略，级联OSD变体可进一步逼近ML界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决BCH码在下一代通信系统中高吞吐量、低延迟译码的需求，传统BP译码计算复杂（tanh/tanh⁻¹函数），且BCH码的代数结构使其难以直接应用并行化BP译码。

Method: 提出准BP译码方案：(1)利用码自同构和优化冗余校验矩阵保持BP的并行结构；(2)提出神经网络变体，用轻量级CNN替代check node更新中的tanh/tanh⁻¹函数，使用三重约束损失函数（非负性和顺序一致性）训练；(3)进一步级联有序统计译码(OSD)变体逼近ML界。

Result: 针对三种BCH码的仿真表明：准BP译码的FER性能与相同码长LDPC码的BP译码差距在0.25dB以内；神经网络变体性能损失可忽略，且能在硬件加速器上稳定部署；级联OSD变体进一步缩小了与ML界的差距。

Conclusion: 提出的方案为下一代通信系统中BCH码的高吞吐量、低延迟译码提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a quasi-BP decoding scheme for BCH codes that preserves the parallelizable structure of belief propagation while exploiting code automorphisms and optimized redundant parity-check matrices. To eliminate the computationally expensive $\tanh$ and $\tanh^{-1}$ functions in check node updates, we further introduce a neural-network-based variant that replaces them with a lightweight convolutional neural network trained under a triple-constraint loss function enforcing non-negativity and order consistency. Simulation results for three BCH codes demonstrate that quasi-BP decoding achieves competitive frame error rate performance, with a gap within 0.25 decibels compared with belief propagation decoding of an LDPC code of similar blocklength. The neural-network-based variant incurs negligible performance loss while enabling stable deployment with arithmetic operations on hardware accelerators. Concatenation with an ordered statistics decoding variant further bridges the gap to the maximum-likelihood bound. Hence, the proposed schemes offer a viable path toward high-throughput, low-latency decoding of BCH codes in next-generation communication systems.

</details>


### [241] [Identification Codes and Post-Shannon Communication: Theory, Architectures, and Emerging Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14666)
*Wafa Labidi,Kumar Nilesh,Johannes Rosenberger,Juan Cabrera,Holger Boche,Christian Deppe,Frank H. P. Fitzek,Marc Geitz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述了由Ahlswede和Dueck提出的身份识别（ID）编码理论，该理论用假设检验替代消息重建，使得可识别消息数量随块长呈双指数增长，特别适用于监控、报警和控制等无需完整消息重建的场景，并讨论了基于该范式的通信架构和应用领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统香农通信范式要求接收端完全重建发送的消息，但在大规模分布式系统中（如监控、报警、控制系统），接收端仅需判断某一特定消息是否被发送，而非解码全部信息。ID编码通过假设检验替代消息重建，实现了可识别消息数量的双指数增长，为后香农通信提供了关键理论基础。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了ID编码的理论基础，总结了该领域的代表性工作，并通过分析多个应用场景（监控系统、专用数据存储、联合识别与感知JIDAS、语义通信、移动网络控制系统、网络化一致性测试系统）以及近期系统概念、工业视角和实现案例，展示ID原理在实用通信系统中的实现途径。

Result: ID编码的核心理论结果是可识别消息数量随块长呈双指数增长，远优于香农通信的指数增长。在应用层面，ID范式在无需完整消息重建的场景中展现出显著优势，已被探索用于多种实际系统，并涌现出相应的系统概念和实现实例。

Conclusion: ID编码不能完全替代传统香农通信，但在监控、报警、控制等特定场景中具有独特优势。本文通过理论梳理和应用案例分析，展示了ID编码作为后香农通信关键构建块的潜力，为未来实用通信系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Identification (ID) coding, introduced by Ahlswede and Dueck, extends Shannon's classical communication paradigm by replacing message reconstruction with hypothesis testing. Instead of decoding the transmitted message, the receiver only decides whether a particular message was sent. A fundamental result of ID theory is the double-exponential growth in the number of identifiable messages with respect to (w.r.t.) the blocklength. This scaling behavior enables fundamentally new communication architectures for large-scale distributed systems and forms a key building block of post-Shannon communication.
  While ID cannot replace classical communication in general, it is particularly well-suited for scenarios in which full message reconstruction is unnecessary, such as monitoring, alarming, and control systems.
  In this survey, we review the theoretical foundations of ID coding and discuss emerging communication architectures and application domains based on this paradigm. Particular emphasis is placed on practical use cases, including monitoring systems, special-purpose data storage, joint identification and sensing (JIDAS), semantic communications, mobile-network control systems and networked consensus testing systems. We further highlight recent system concepts, industrial perspectives, and implementation examples that illustrate how ID-based principles can be realized in practical communication systems.

</details>


### [242] [Statistics of the Compression Ratio of a Variable-to-Variable Code: Exact Moments and Asymptotic Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14676)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文推导了V2V变长码在有限样本下压缩比R_n的精确矩公式、Edgeworth近似、偏差常数和方差常数，并将其推广至马尔可夫源；同时在编码理论层面建立了有限状态编码器框架，给出了Kraft不等式下界，并通过对偏差系数的结构分解解释了Khodak码性能更优的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2V变长码的渐近率ρ仅在无穷多短语时成立，实际应用中有限样本下的压缩比R_n的分布特性尚不明确，缺乏精确的矩公式和有限样本分布逼近工具。

Method: 1) 利用单短语(L,ℓ)的矩生成函数，推导R_n各阶矩的闭式一维积分表达式；2) 基于矩推导Edgeworth近似逼近R_n的累积分布函数；3) 应用Laplace积分方法导出偏差常数和方差常数的闭式公式；4) 将V2V码建模为有限状态编码器，推广Kraft不等式；5) 对偏差系数进行V2F/F2V/V2V的结构分解。

Result: 得到了R_n所有整数阶矩的闭式公式；Edgeworth近似优于CLT近似；给出了偏差常数C和方差常数的显式闭式公式；将结果推广到马尔可夫源；对Khodak码的偏差分解表明其更优性能体现在更小的偏差常数上。

Conclusion: 本文为V2V变长码提供了完整的有限样本分析框架，理论和编码两方面结果统一了V2F/F2V/V2V三类码的偏差分析，并为Khodak码的优越性提供了理论解释。

Abstract: A variable-to-variable (V2V) length code parses a source sequence into phrases of variable length and maps each phrase to a binary codeword of, generally, a different random length. After encoding $n$ phrases, the realized compression ratio $R_n=Λ_n/Σ_n$ -- total codeword length over total source-symbol count -- is the finite-sample counterpart of the code's asymptotic rate $ρ$, to which it converges only as $n\to\infty$. This paper first derives exact formulas for all integer moments of $R_n$ for a given discrete memoryless source (DMS). Specifically, we obtain a closed-form formula for every moment $\E\{R_n^k\}$ as a one-dimensional integral involving only single-phrase moment generating functions of the pair $(L,\ell)$ -- the phrase length, in source symbols, and codeword length, in bits. From these moments we derive an Edgeworth approximation to the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of $R_n$ that is substantially more accurate than the central limit theorem (CLT) approximation. Using the Laplace method of integration, we also derive explicit closed-form formulas for the bias constant $C=\lim_{n\to\infty}n(\E\{R_n\}-ρ)$ and for the variance constant $\lim_{n\to\infty}n\cdot\Var\{R_n\}$. The analysis extends to Markov sources via state-indexed matrices with a redundancy formula obtained in closed form. On the coding-theoretic side, we cast V2V length codes as finite-state encoders and apply a generalized Kraft inequality for a compression-rate lower bound, and give a structural decomposition of the bias coefficient that separates cleanly across variable-to-fixed (V2F) length codes, fixed-to-variable (F2V) length codes, and V2V length codes. Applied to the Khodak code of Bugeaud, Drmota, and Szpankowski, this decomposition shows that its improved performance is reflected in its smaller bias constant.

</details>


### [243] [Random Spherical Codes at High SNR: Error Transitions, Fixed-Error Data Rates, and Converse Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14768)
*Nikola Zlatanov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文刻画了高信噪比、固定块长度且码本大小随信噪比增长条件下，随机球面码本在实加性高斯白噪声信道上的误差概率相变特性，并由此推导出可达速率的渐近展开及其与逆率界的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高信噪比固定块长度且码本大小随信噪比增长的场景下，系统性地刻画随机球面码本的误差概率行为及其可达速率与理论逆率界之间的差距。

Method: 利用随机球面码本在高信噪比下的误差概率相变分析，通过球面本征维度和码本增长尺度刻画临界行为，并基于该相变律逆推得到可达速率的高信噪比展开式。

Result: 随机球面码本的误差概率存在以码本增长尺度为参数的相变现象；可达速率与逆率界的比值趋近于1，但加法差距收敛到与块长度和错误概率相关的正极限；该加法差距随块长度增大而消失，但特定可靠性标度下错误概率的下降可阻止该差距在块长度趋于无穷时消失。

Conclusion: 该研究从有限块长度与渐近联合的视角揭示了随机球面码本在高信噪比下的可达速率与逆率界之间的精确关系，尤其刻画了加法差距随块长度和错误概率变化的完整行为。

Abstract: This paper characterizes random spherical codebooks over the real additive white Gaussian noise channel in the high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime when the blocklength is fixed and the codebook size grows with SNR. In this regime, the random spherical ensemble exhibits a sharp error-probability transition governed by the intrinsic dimension of the sphere and the codebook-growth scale. Below the critical codebook-growth scale, the ensemble-average error probability vanishes; at the critical scale, it converges to a nontrivial limit; and above that scale, it approaches one. By inverting this transition law, we obtain the high-SNR expansion of the ensemble-achievable data rate for a prescribed error probability. This rate has the same leading high-SNR growth as the corresponding converse rate bound, while reliability enters through the constant-order terms. Consequently, the ratio of the ensemble-achievable rate to the converse rate bound tends to one as the SNR increases. Their additive difference, however, generally approaches a positive blocklength- and reliability-dependent limit. We characterize this limiting rate-bound gap jointly as a function of blocklength and error probability. For every fixed error probability, the gap vanishes as the blocklength increases. We further identify the reliability scalings under which a decreasing error probability prevents the additive gap from vanishing in the large-blocklength limit.

</details>


### [244] [Lossy compression of weighted graph adjacency matrices by transform coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14834)
*Kenta Yanagiya,Junya Hara,Hiroshi Higashi,Yuichi Tanaka,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向加权图的压缩框架，其中图拓扑无损传输，边权重有损压缩。通过将原图转换为线图，将边权重视为线图上的图信号，利用图滤波器组进行变换、量化和熵编码。同时提出了基于线图的边平滑度指标，可无需转换直接计算，用于衡量压缩难度。实验验证了该方法优于传统矩阵预处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权图压缩中，边权重之间的潜在关系具有模糊性，导致有损压缩面临挑战。现有方法未能有效利用边间的结构关联，因此需要一种能捕捉边关系并指导压缩的框架。

Method: 1) 将原无权图转换为线图，其节点对应原图边，边编码原边间的关系；2) 将边权重向量视为线图上的图信号；3) 对边权重信号应用线图上的图滤波器组进行变换；4) 对变换系数进行量化和熵编码；5) 提出基于线图边平滑度的度量，无需显式转换即可评估压缩难度。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在边权重的有损压缩性能上优于现有的矩阵预处理方法。

Conclusion: 线图变换有效利用了边间关系来指导加权图的有损压缩；所提出的边平滑度度量可作为压缩性能的预判指标，为加权图压缩提供了理论洞见和实践方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a compression framework for weighted graphs in which the graph topology is transmitted losslessly and edge weights are compressed lossily. A challenge in the lossy compression of edge weights is that the underlying relationships between edges are ambiguous. To address this issue, we first transform the unweighted graph into the corresponding line graph, whose nodes represent the edges of the original graph and whose edges encode the relationships between them. The line graph transform allows us to regard edge weights as a graph signal defined on the line graph. Instead of transmitting the edge-weight vector, we first transform it with a graph filter bank on the line graph. Then, quantization and entropy coding are performed on the transformed coefficients of the edge weight vector. In addition to the lossy compression method, we formalize edge smoothness on the line graph and show that it serves as a measure of the difficulty of compression. The proposed smoothness measure can be easily calculated without converting to a line graph. This provides insight into the expected compression performance of a given weighted graph. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data validate the effectiveness of the proposed method by comparing it with existing matrix preprocessing methods.

</details>


### [245] [Covering Sequences and Covering-Sequences Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14840)
*Tuvi Etzion*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了(n,R)-覆盖序列和(n,m,R)-覆盖序列码，利用汉明码构造了短长度、少序列数的覆盖序列和码，并构建了渐近最优的小半径序列以及适用于大半径的码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖序列和覆盖序列码是构建最优覆盖序列的基本模块，本文旨在利用汉明码构造短长度、少量序列的覆盖序列和码，并探索渐近最优的构造方法。

Method: 利用汉明码构造(n,R)-覆盖序列和(n,m,R)-覆盖序列码；对于小半径，构造短长度和少量序列的码；对于素数幂大小的字母表，构造渐近最优的小半径序列；并推广到大半径情形。

Result: 成功构造了短长度的覆盖序列和码，序列数相对较少；构造了渐近最优的小半径序列（素数幂字母表）；大半径下也构造了有趣的码。

Conclusion: 汉明码是构造覆盖序列和覆盖序列码的有效工具，能够在不同半径下获得短长度、少序列数或渐近最优的构造。

Abstract: An $(n,R)$-covering sequence is a cyclic sequence whose consecutive $n$-tuples form a code of length $n$ and covering radius $R$. An $(n,m,R)$-covering-sequences code is a set of cyclic sequences of length $m$, whose consecutive $n$-tuples form a code of length $n$ and covering radius $R$. These codes are the best building blocks for $(n,R)$-covering sequences. We show, for small radii, how the Hamming code can be used to construct such sequences of short length and such codes with a relatively small number of sequences and a total number of codewords. Sequences with small radius whose length approaches asymptotically to optimality are constructed, especially for an alphabet of prime power size large enough. With the same construction, interesting codes are also constructed for larger radii.

</details>


### [246] [Quantum XYZ Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14988)
*Alessio Baldelli,Davide Orsucci,Francisco Lázaro,Massimo Battaglioni*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出量子XYZ稳定子码，其校验矩阵由三个两两正交的二进制校验矩阵（对应X、Y、Z型稳定子生成元）构成，并系统分析了该结构何时退化为CSS码、推导了量子最小距离的上下界，给出了有限长量子LDPC码的构造，仿真表明XYZ qLDPC码在退极化噪声下优于同类CSS qLDPC码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CSS码虽代数结构便利，但引入非CSS约束有助于抑制低权重逻辑算子和提升有限长译码性能，因此需要构建并分析更一般的非CSS稳定子码框架。

Method: 定义XYZ稳定子码的PCM由三个两两正交的二进制PCM构成；通过代数和秩条件刻画其退化为CSS码的条件；推导最小距离的上下界；利用交子集和准对偶码族构造稀疏有限长qLDPC实例；采用退极化信道下的四元置信传播译码进行仿真。

Result: 给出了XYZ码退化为CSS码的判别条件；获得了量子最小距离的上下界（含混合Pauli逻辑算子）；包含了已知的非CSS拓扑码XYZ²六边形码；构造的新qLDPC码在相似参数下优于代表性CSS qLDPC码。

Conclusion: XYZ稳定子码框架统一并拓展了非CSS稳定子码的构造，为设计性能更优的有限长量子LDPC码提供了新途径。

Abstract: Stabilizer codes are often constructed within the Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) framework, where two mutually orthogonal binary classical codes define $X$ and $Z$-type stabilizer generators. While this structure is algebraically convenient, additional non-CSS constraints may help suppress low-weight logical operators and improve decoding performance in the finite-length regime. We thus introduce quantum XYZ stabilizer codes, whose parity-check matrix (PCM) is built from three pairwise orthogonal binary PCMs associated with $X$-, $Y$-, and $Z$-type stabilizer generators. A nontrivial point is that an XYZ code instance is not automatically genuinely non-CSS: the same stabilizer group may admit a CSS generating set. We characterize this collapse, obtaining algebraic and rank conditions for deciding when the $Y$-type checks are redundant and when they define genuinely non-CSS stabilizer constraints. We also derive upper and lower bounds on the quantum minimum distance, including bounds for mixed Pauli logical operators. The novel framework includes a known non-CSS topological code, namely the XYZ$^2$ hexagonal code, and yields also sparse finite-length quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) constructions from intersecting-subset and quasi-dyadic code families. Simulations under depolarizing code-capacity noise and quaternary belief propagation decoding show that the proposed XYZ qLDPC instances can outperform representative CSS qLDPC instances with similar finite-length parameters.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Accounting for Hysteresis and Eddy Currents in Finite Element Simulations of Ferromagnetic Laminated Cores using a Recurrent Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14321)
*Florent Purnode,Louis Denis,François Henrotte,Gilles Louppe,Christophe Geuzaine*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出用循环神经网络替代叠片铁芯的磁滞和涡流模型，集成到2D有限元仿真中，计算成本仅为无磁滞仿真的约2倍，精度高且开源可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将磁滞和涡流纳入叠片铁芯电机有限元仿真计算量极大（比无磁滞仿真高几个数量级），不切实际；而仅考虑磁饱和的简化模型在复杂工况下越来越不准确，需要高效且精确的替代方案。

Method: 训练循环神经网络作为各向同性叠片铁芯材料模型的代理模型，基于磁矢量势公式将其集成到二维磁动力学有限元仿真中，网络使用人工生成的代表电机仿真中磁场序列的数据集进行训练。

Result: 该方法与参考叠片铁芯模型吻合良好，计算成本仅为无磁滞仿真的约2倍，远低于传统全物理建模方法。

Conclusion: RNN代理模型能高效准确地替代叠片铁芯的磁滞和涡流建模，易于集成到现有计算框架，并已在GitLab上开源。

Abstract: Incorporating hysteresis and eddy currents into finite element simulations of laminated-core electrical machines is computationally challenging. Resolving the fields inside the laminations at each integration point and at every nonlinear iteration leads to computational costs several orders of magnitude higher than anhysteretic simulations, making such approaches impractical for design applications. Conversely, simplified models accounting only for magnetic saturation are becoming increasingly inadequate as electrical machine topologies and operating conditions grow in complexity. In this context, machine learning surrogate modeling has emerged as a promising alternative, offering efficient and accurate approximations of complex electromagnetic behaviors. In this paper, a recurrent neural network is trained as a surrogate of a laminated-core material model for an isotropic laminated core, and is integrated into realistic two-dimensional magnetodynamic finite element simulations based on a magnetic vector potential formulation. The proposed approach achieves excellent agreement with the reference laminated-core model while limiting the computational cost to about twice that of an anhysteretic simulation. By training the recurrent neural network on a sufficiently diverse set of artificially generated magnetic field sequences designed to mimic those encountered in electrical machine simulations, the proposed approach can be readily applied across a wide range of finite element simulations. Furthermore, the trained surrogate model is provided as a standalone component that can be easily incorporated into existing computational frameworks. It is publicly available at https://gitlab.onelab.info/getdp/lamnet.

</details>


### [248] [Model-Informed Joint Material-Structural Optimization of Hard-Magnetic Soft Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14397)
*Ian Galloway,Prashant K. Jha*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于模型的框架，用于硬磁软材料的预测分析与优化设计。该框架将经典夹杂模型、Hill自洽模型和约束运动学模型统一到有效剪切模量框架中，得到21种本构模型，并基于实验数据选出Mooney关系为最优。在此基础上，开发了联合材料-结构优化框架，可同时设计结构密度、磁颗粒体积分数和剩余磁化方向，产生非直觉的设计结果。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬磁软材料在软体机器人、自适应结构和仿生系统中具有重要应用，但准确预测其场驱动变形需要有效的结构-性能关系，而优化设计则需要同时控制结构密度、磁颗粒分布和剩余磁化方向。现有研究缺乏统一的预测框架和联合优化方法。

Method: (1) 将经典刚性夹杂关系、Hill自洽模型和约束运动学模型统一到有效剪切模量框架，结合7种剪切模量关系和3种应变能密度函数得到21种本构模型，并通过实验应力-应变数据选择最优关系；(2) 基于选定本构模型，建立联合材料-结构优化框架，同时优化结构密度、磁颗粒体积分数和剩余磁化方向，支持旋转、平移和恢复变形等不同设计目标。

Result: 应变能密度函数形式对驱动变形问题影响较小，而有效剪切模量关系在磁材料覆盖高变形区域时显著影响变形。Mooney关系与实验数据吻合最佳。联合优化框架成功处理了不同主动设计场、目标和单/多载荷工况，产生了非直觉的最优设计。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型-informed框架可有效预测硬磁软材料的变形行为并实现最优设计，代码已开源在CEADpx/top_optim仓库中，为软体机器人和自适应结构的设计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: This work develops a model-informed framework for predictive analysis and optimal design of hard-magnetic soft materials (hMSMs). These materials undergo contact-free, field-driven deformation, making them attractive for soft robotics, adaptive structures, and bio-inspired systems. Accurate prediction requires effective structure--property relations, while optimal design requires simultaneous control of structural density, magnetic particle distribution, and remanent magnetization direction. To address these issues, this work makes two main contributions. First, classical rigid-inclusion relations, a Hill self-consistent relation, and constrained-kinematics models are placed into a unified effective shear-modulus framework for particle-filled elastomers. With one default control relation, seven shear-modulus relations are combined with three strain-energy density functions to obtain 21 constitutive models. The results show that the strain-energy density form has a relatively small effect for the actuation problems considered, whereas the effective shear-modulus relation can significantly affect deformation when magnetic material overlaps with highly deforming regions. Experimental stress--strain data are then used to select a representative shear-modulus relation, with the Mooney relation giving the best overall agreement. Second, using the selected constitutive model, a joint material--structural optimization framework is developed for simultaneous design of structural density, magnetic particle volume fraction, and remanent magnetization direction. Rotational, translational, and restorative examples show that the framework handles different active design fields, objectives, and single- or multi-load-case formulations, producing non-intuitive hMSM designs with prescribed deformation responses.
